(b) **The list managers' Restore** (task types, templates, the 1.4 list manager, custom fields) is a ghost button with a busy flag, not `useAction`: no working label, not disabled offline, no "Couldn't reach MaxOff" (PR #22's sweep covers only `primary` / `submit`). (c) **An Admin may decide their own suggestion** (the SQL's rule: they see it and hold `task_requests.decide`), and it counts in their "suggested tasks to decide"; if the owner wants someone else to decide an Admin's suggestion, that is a new rule in `task_request_convert` / `_decline` (an owner question for `/review-phase 4`). (d) Suggested tasks keeps the latest 50 decided ones and no more (no paging); a withdrawn one shows no date (`decided_at` is only for converted / declined). (e) ~~**The held-skeleton check of an Admin's `/today`** lost its race once in 20 loads~~ **done** (4C review M1: held by its data, `e2e/loading-screens.spec.ts`). (f) **The Owner's team month at large text (3b.4, not 4C):** a row whose unpaid expense line is long ("₹1,200.50 to pay · 1 claim") reaches past a 375/430 phone at 130% and 200%, because its trailing part is `CARD_ROW_TRAILING` (`shrink-0`) and cannot wrap; `mobile.spec`'s `/reports/month` check goes red whenever `expenses.spec` approved its claim first (order-dependent; reproduced on `e8764c2`). Let the trailing part wrap (`min-w-0`, the amount on its own line) in `month-summary.tsx`. (g) ~~**The held-skeleton checks of fast pages race**~~ **done** (4C review M1: the page's data is held at the e2e server's Supabase proxy, so no page answers before its shell; `e2e/loading-screens.spec.ts`). (h) **The 30 s test timeout and this container:** `expenses.spec:69` and `extra-work.spec:58` take 27–32 s here (the same on the pre-4C commit), so a full local run reds on them; CI's clean runner is the proof (2.6: never raise a timeout). (i) **`data-slot` overrides on modals** (4B's task dialogs `task-form-dialog`, `task-done-dialog`, `task-comment-dialog`, `task-stage-dialog`, `task-approver-dialog`; 4C's ⋯ sheets `task-type-actions`, `task-template-actions`) replace the primitive's slot and so drop `[data-slot$="dialog-content"]` / `sheet-content`'s overscroll containment; the specs could find them by role or a `data-testid`-like attribute instead.

(L4) **`coordinator-dialogs.tsx:54` has no `.catch`**: offline, `getCoordinatorChoices` never settles and the dialog says "Loading…" forever; the same for the handover choices and the open-task count read behind Invite as employee: catch and show "Couldn't reach MaxOff" with a retry. (L5) **`modules/team` imports `modules/tasks`** (`src/modules/team/actions/members.ts:15`, `countOpenAssignments` for Invite as employee's warning): a module reaching into another's index; compose it in `app/` (the page reads the count and hands it to the dialog) instead. (L7) **Unindexed sorts:** `listFinishedTaskRows` (`order by updated_at desc limit 300` over completed / cancelled tasks) and the decided-requests read (`neq state pending order by updated_at desc limit 50`) have no index on the sort; run EXPLAIN on `measure-seed` volumes when the tables grow (ARCHITECTURE §19: an index only with an EXPLAIN that uses it). (L8) **`/today` and `/my-day` stand at 783.5 of 785 KB**: 1.5 KB left for 5.1's bell and Realtime; plan what leaves the shell first. (L10) **Offer a reassign before Invite as employee:** its warning counts the person's open tasks nobody can act on until they accept; a "Reassign them first" link to the filtered full list would close the loop (an owner question).
- **`main` merge, later items (2026-09-29):** (a) **A prefetch the proxy redirects leaves a stale redirect behind:** the fetch follows the proxy's 307s without the `_rsc` marker, Next answers the target with its cache-busting 307 (`/my-day` → `/my-day?_rsc=…`, no `Cache-Control`, no `Vary`), the browser keeps it under the page's address, and Chrome replays it on a back/forward load of that address (the app lands on `?_rsc=`). The sign-in page's Forgot link, re-prefetched with the new session, was the one trigger in the suite (no longer prefetched, `63371f9`); the error screens' "Go home" (`<Link href="/">`, `src/app/error.tsx`, `src/app/(app)/error.tsx`) is another (`/` → home): `prefetch={false}` there too, or raise it with Next (that 307 should carry `Vary` or `no-store`). (b) **`/people` starts `listDirectory()` before the role is known** (PR #22, §19): only the Owner and Admins pass its check, but a Staff member opening `/people` by address starts the 2 s whole-directory read ("4B mechanics" (1)) before the redirect; start it behind the check, or rewrite `app.directory_visible()` set-based (4C). (c) `e2e/pull-to-refresh.spec.ts` now runs serially (its tests share one seeded person per project; `0c52d2a`).

(b) The task page starts the edit dialog's setup (six reads) for every manager's view, though few open Edit: move it behind the first open (a server action) if the page's reads ever queue. (c) Settings → Task types (the Owner's editor with `task_type_move()`) and per-type task fields in Settings → Custom fields are not built (4C or with 5.3's reminder editor; "Last completed: unit 4B"). (d) A stage cannot be renamed from the task page (remove and add); the guard allows a manager's rename, a small dialog if the pilot asks. (e) `MODAL_SURFACE`'s `duration-200` without a `transition-property` transitions every property (the padding eases after a text-size change, 4B mechanics (4)); name the animated properties if a real screen ever shows it. (f) The history lists the latest 50 entries (`ACTIVITY_LIMIT`) with no "show earlier"; a busy task may need paging. (g) A stage tick's audit row carries no stage id or name (4B mechanics (6)); if the history must always name it, `app.task_stages_guard()` could set an audit override with the stage's name on the API path (a migration).

- **4B later items (review)** (2026-09-29, the orchestrator's independent review of 4B; M1 and S1–S12 are fixed by the 4B fixer, S8 as the interim below, S9 left as built for the owner; line numbers refer to `db70bad`):

- **4A later items (review)** (2026-09-29, the orchestrator's independent review of 4A; M1, M2, S1–S4 are fixed in `20260929104507_phase4a_review_fixes`):
  - (L1) Adding the approving Admin as an assignee while the task is `submitted` sticks it: nothing re-evaluates the route (`20260929053636_task_transitions.sql:915-921`), `task_review` refuses the Admin as an assignee (`:701-703`, untested) and the Owner gets `INVALID_STATE` (`:696-698`); the Owner's way out today is `task_set_approver(id, null)`. Fix: re-run the set-approver logic in `task_update_assignment`, or refuse that change while `submitted`.

  - (L6) pgTAP thin spots: **done in `34`** (the approving Admin and a co-assignee read `task_reviews`, the approving Admin reads `task_warnings`, the coordinator's untick, `task_cancel` from `submitted` and `admin_approved`, the Owner comments).
  - (L7) `task_set_approver` at `admin_approved` naming a new approver keeps `admin_step = 'none'` (`20260929053636:1131`) when the task had none: the record says no Admin step although one is named now; decide whether it should read `required` (the step is behind the task either way).

- **4A, "later" items (2026-09-29, the in-session architecture review):** (a) `app.directory_visible()` evaluates `app.task_visible()` per task per directory row for Staff; rewrite as one join on the caller's own assignee / coordinator rows if `member_directory` ever slows. (b) `task_types.position` has no move function yet (the grant keeps `position` out of the API, as list_items); the Settings → Task types screen (4B/4C or 5.x) adds `task_type_move()` with the `settings.manage` check, mirroring `list_item_move()`. (c) The `client_labels` view now serves Staff the labels of their tasks; `app.file_visible()`'s client-logo branch already follows it, so a Staff member sees the logo of a labelled client (intended, ADR-0005) — worth a pgTAP line when 8.x touches files. (d) A deactivated assignee stays on open tasks "flagged" (WORKFLOWS §1b): nothing flags it yet; 4.5's lists should mark an inactive assignee. (e) `TeamMember` lacks `engagement`; the Owner's `/people/[id]` would show Leave / Attendance / Month tabs for a freelancer ("4A mechanics" (8)); 4C. (f) **`/reports/month` at 200% text overflows on the unpaid line** (`team-month-unpaid`, "₹1,200.50 to pay · 1 claim" beside the chevron on a 375px phone) whenever a member has an approved, unpaid claim; `mobile.spec` "/reports/month: fits at 130% and 200%" catches it only when `expenses.spec` was interrupted before its Mark paid / withdraw steps (the 3cB run saw it once too). A 3b.4 layout fix (let the line wrap), not 4A's. (g) **Local full e2e runs took 16 min on 2026-09-29 (10.8 before)** on the restarted cloud container (load average 15–19 on 4 cores): the two long serial tests (`expenses.spec.ts:69`, `extra-work.spec.ts:58`, ~200 steps each) reach their last steps at 30 s and hit the test timeout on every project, with or without the 4A migrations (a control run on the pre-4A schema failed the same way). Not a code red and no timeout was raised; if it recurs on a normal day, split each into two serial tests (the member's part, the Owner's part) rather than lengthen the timeout.
- **Tap feedback and performance, deferred (2026-09-28, `feat/tap-feedback`, not a roadmap task; ARCHITECTURE §14.1, §14.2 i, §19):** (a) **`useActionState` forms still send a network failure to the error boundary** (sign-in, forgot and set password, Settings → Company, Thresholds, a list's Add, Expenses → receipt amount): they have the pending state and the one-request rule, but not "Couldn't reach MaxOff" with Retry, which needs a `useActionState`-aware wrapper (the typed values are lost today when the connection drops mid-submit). (b) **`use-member-dialogs.tsx`** ("Copy invite link" and "Reactivate" from a person's ⋯ menu) still runs its actions in a bare transition: the failure line has nowhere visible to go (the invite-link dialog belongs to another component, and Retry would issue a new link, which revokes the previous one), so it needs a small design decision first. (c) **Idempotency keys for creates:** Retry after a lost reply can create twice (a new client, contact, holiday or claim); state transitions refuse a repeat already, and comp-leave grants carry a request key. (d) **Row-link prefetch volume:** every visible `DrillLink` prefetches its route up to `loading.tsx` (40 clients = 40 small requests on a slow link); watch it on the phone walk, and consider `prefetch={false}` on long lists if it competes with the tap's own request. (e) **`client_brand`'s RLS calls `app.client_visible()` per row** (0.24 ms each, 10 ms for 40 clients' logos, linear): fine now, revisit with a set-based policy past a few hundred clients (touches RLS: owner approval). (f) **The cold start** of a Worker isolate (the first-load TTFB on the preview) is 6.5b's. **v1.0.0 review, "later" items (2026-09-29, architecture-reviewer on `origin/main...feat/tap-feedback`; the must-fix and every should-fix were fixed before the push, and three small items too: a cancelled touch no longer counts as a pull's release, `pendingLabel` is a string, and the Me copy no longer mentions pulling on desktop):** (g) **the pull spinner ends when the refresh's response headers arrive**, not when the new data is on screen, and another screen fetch for the same address (refresh on return) can end it early: report settlement after the body, or keep the spinner until the next commit after it. (h) **`pending-buttons.test.ts` only sweeps `primary`/`submit`**: a `strong` button with `commits` or a destructive button that commits directly escapes it. (i) **Fixed sleeps in "didn't happen" e2e checks** (`tap-feedback.spec` sheet-close, `pull-to-refresh.spec` throttle and sheet checks): prefer a positive signal (request idle) to `waitForTimeout`; the 100–150 ms expectations are tighter than default, watch them for CI noise. (j) **Speculative reads a role can't use** (an Admin's four Approvals lists, `getTodayPeople` for Admin and Staff, the Owner's comp balance in the Leave layout): measure their database cost on the preview, and note that `startEarly` keeps their failures out of Sentry. (k) **Head-script fragility:** `pre-hydration.ts` embeds `startsNavigation.toString()`; a unit test that the **built** script parses would pin it against a toolchain change. **Second review pass, "later" items (2026-09-29, architecture-reviewer on `origin/main...73b6835`; its must-fix and every should-fix were fixed before the push, and `resetKey` narrowed to plain values):** (l) **URL encoding mismatch in `NavProgress`**: `searchParams.delete("_rsc")` and `useSearchParams().toString()` re-encode a query form-style while Next keeps `%20`, `:`, `,`, `/` as written, so with such a query a refresh would count as a navigation (bar held to 25 s), an overlay closing would flash the bar, and a pull's spinner would wait its full 15 s; MaxOff's own URLs are safe today; strip `_rsc` by string and normalise both sides. (m) **`begin()` resets `elsewhere`**, correct only because Next awaits a digest before it fetches; on an insecure origin (a phone on http over the LAN) the fetch can start before the click listener runs; don't reset it while a counted fetch for another address is in flight. (n) **A redirect back to the same address** (`/leave?page=N` → `/leave`) never sets `moved`: the bar runs to 25 s; add a fallback finish `NAV_SETTLE_MS` after the answer. (o) **A router-started `router.replace`** (the client and person ⋯ menus): Retry falls back to `router.push`, and after 25 s to `location.assign`; both add an entry. (p) **`attendance_decide('correct')` accepts an identical repeat** (any state → corrected): a Retry after a lost reply records a second correction event (and, from 5.1, a second notification); a request key would close it (documented in ARCHITECTURE §14.1).
- **3c review, "later" item (2026-09-29, found by the CI-red harness, not fixed):** **`/settings/company` reaches past a 375px phone at 200% system text**, the settled page, not its skeleton: the logo card (`company-logo-card.tsx`, `flex items-center gap-4 p-4`) keeps the 192px logo beside a `min-w-0 flex-1` column that is left 21px wide, and the Change logo button inside its bare `<div>` keeps its 157px, so the layout viewport widens and the sticky Save bar and the bottom bar follow it (`scrollWidth` 446). `/settings/company` is in `SCREENS` (100%) but not in `LARGE_TEXT_SCREENS`. Fix: let the card wrap (`flex-wrap`, the button under the text) and add the route to the large-text sweep.

- **Phase 3 review, "later" items (2026-09-27, `/review-phase 3`: architecture-reviewer + security audit on `main...phase-3`; every must-fix and should-fix was fixed in the review, see "Phase 3 review decisions"):** (a) ~~**SVG sanitiser gaps** (low impact behind `<img>`, the sandbox CSP and the attachment disposition): an `xmlns:f` alias of the XLink namespace lets `<use f:href="https://…">` through (treat any attribute whose local name is `href` as a reference, drop `xmlns:*` other than the exact XLink URI); a CSS escape (`style="background:\75rl(…)"`) passes the `url(` test (drop a `style` holding a backslash, or `style` altogether); `processEntities:false` on parse with escaping on build turns `&amp;` into `&amp;amp;` (parse with entities on).~~ **Done 2026-09-28 (`fix/storage-hardening`):** any attribute whose local name is `href` is a reference and only `href` / `xlink:href` are kept, `xmlns:*` other than the exact XLink URI is dropped, any attribute value holding a backslash is dropped, and parsing decodes entities (numeric references included) so `&amp;` is escaped once and `&#117;rl(` is caught; unit tests for each. (b) `clients.created_at` / `created_by` and `client_contacts.created_at` may be set by the caller on insert (cosmetic; force them in the insert guards when `not app.in_transition()`). (c) `/api/cron/storage-cleanup`: the `a.length === b.length` short-circuit before `timingSafeEqual` reveals the secret's length (compare SHA-256 digests of both). (d) ~~`getDownloadUrl`'s presigned GET has no `response-content-type` override (the stored type comes from the uploader's unsigned PUT; harmless on the R2 origin with the attachment disposition; add `response-content-type` = the row's validated mime).~~ **Done 2026-09-28 (`fix/storage-hardening`):** `presignGet` always signs `response-content-disposition: attachment` and `response-content-type` (`downloadContentType()`: an image, video, audio or PDF type as stored on the row, anything else `application/octet-stream`); checked against MinIO. (e) `presignPut` does not sign `content-length`: an oversized PUT is caught only by `completeUpload`'s HEAD check (then deleted); signing it would let the bucket refuse it. (f) One bad brand element blanked the whole list in `parseBrandColors` / `parseBrandFonts` (now unreachable: the database checks the shape since `20260927152618`; filter invalid elements anyway if a future writer bypasses it). (g) `storage_key` is readable by whoever reads the file row; keys are no longer choosable (`20260927144619`) and are derivable from visible data, so this is hygiene only. (h) `preview.yml` still carries its own copy of the migration-listing parse; fold it onto `scripts/staging-migrations.sh`.

(b) "Assign Admin" with no other active Admin stays an active red button that only says "Choose an Admin"; disable or hide it. (c) `DataTable` reads the view from the URL only on mount; resync on an external back/forward to a kept-alive page. (d) a client's tabs paint before `loadClient` resolves, so an unknown or another Admin's client flashes tabs before the 404. (e) `listClientSummaries` / `getClient` select `*`, which carries the `search` tsvector; list the columns. (f) The reviewer's "Admin Profile skeleton shows 1 fact line" was checked and kept: the directory has no dates, so an Admin sees Phone only. (g) The Overview skeleton draws the Drive/Call buttons whatever the data (they depend on the client). (h) Last write wins between two people editing the **same** client field (no optimistic concurrency); add `updated_at` checks if the pilot shows it.
- **3A review, "later" items (2026-09-27, architecture-reviewer on `main...phase-3`; must-fix and should-fix were fixed in the 3.3 commit):** (a) ~~Orphaned `ready` files are never cleaned~~ **done in the 3B review fixes** (owner decision 2026-09-27: unreferenced `ready` originals after 7 days, previews follow; migration `20260927130730`, pgTAP `19`). (b) **The presigned PUT signs neither `Content-Length` nor `Content-Type`** (`core/storage/adapter.ts`): a member could PUT a larger object than announced until `completeUpload` rejects the mismatch or cleanup runs; sign `content-length` into the presigned PUT (aws4fetch `signQuery` with headers) by the time phase 5's 100 MB uploads arrive. (d) SVG sanitiser edge cases (`core/storage/svg.ts`): an element off the allow-list is dropped with its children (a logo wrapped in `<a>` vanishes; unwrap `a` / `switch` instead), and ~~parse `processEntities: false` + build `processEntities: true` double-escapes `&amp;` in `<title>` / `<text>`~~ (**done 2026-09-28**, `fix/storage-hardening`). (e) pgTAP 18 gaps left: an Admin / Owner updating another member's `files.name` (RLS `files_update_own`); a cross-org check of `files_reference_guard` on `client_brand` (needs a second organization fixture). (f) The upload sheet's footer puts the destructive Remove beside the red Save (`image-upload-sheet.tsx`); verify the `MODAL_FOOTER` layout at 375 px on the phase-3 phone walk and move Remove above the picker if they are adjacent (§14.1). (g) `storage.spec.ts` proves the logo upload on desktop only (one organization row); the photo flow and the sheet's back order run on both phone projects; part of the phone walk. (h) 3.1: `client_close(reason)` stores `meta.reason` on the `clients` activity entry; the owner settled it in the 3B review (Owner-only, as built on screen); the API hardening is under Ideas / tech debt.
- **Guard the CI e2e job against midnight IST (2026-09-27):** the first CI run on `main` after the phase-2 merge (`13b2af6`, run 36262306586) went red with 28 failures, 25 of them `mobile.spec` in the last project, on a tree byte-identical to `30de819`, which had passed playwright twice on the branch. Its e2e step ran **18:22–18:36 UTC and crossed 18:30 UTC = 00:00 IST**, the known limit from 2.6 (the day flips mid-run, every signed-in member meets the day gate again, screens "don't load"). Nobody schedules CI, a push does, so the limit needs a mechanism: make the `playwright` job **wait** when the clock is within ~15 minutes before 18:30 UTC (a `sleep` until 18:31 with a notice, cheap on a free runner) or fail fast with a message naming the window, and put the same guard in `/run-phase`'s local runs. Until then: a red on `main` whose e2e step spans 18:30 UTC is re-run, not investigated (`gh run rerun <id> --failed`). **Widen the window to 18:29 UTC (23:59 IST):** the `absent_check` job fires then and can propose 200+ absences into a running suite's database, which pushes today's rows off the capped Approvals list (the first failure of run 36262306586 was exactly at 18:29:11 UTC).
- **Phase 2 review, "later" items (2026-09-26):** (a) the jobs' 7-day catch-up reads membership as of the run, not per date (`20260925010221:227-236`): a member deactivated on D-3 and reactivated on D gets proposed absences for D-3..D-1; revisit when member lifecycle history exists. (b) The Admin's `/approvals` placeholder and the Today / My Day placeholder tiles have generic skeletons; 4.5, 6.1 and 6.2 must each replace them with a tracing of the real screen. (c) `EditableRecord`'s `autoFocus` pops the keyboard on Edit; iOS's fixed sticky bar above the keyboard is device-dependent (on the device list). (d) No cap on open leave requests per member (`leave_submit` accepts thousands of one-day rows; the Approvals list is capped at 200 on screen, so noise, not outage): consider `> 20 submitted → CONFLICT`. (e) `attendance_flag_overtime` accepts any own day in any state with no signal to the Owner (matches DATA-MODEL; consider `work_date >= today - 1` or a "flagged after decision" mark in the history). (f) RLS is enabled but never `force`d on any table (phase 1 too); only a future job running as the table owner without a `where member_id` would notice — decide a convention. (g) `workflow_dispatch: staging-migrations` in `preview.yml` lets any branch push migrations to the shared staging database; an environment reviewer rule on `staging` would make it explicit. (h) `app.attendance_logout()` and `attendance_flag_overtime_today()` each state the same "which day" rule; if it ever changes, change both (or factor a helper then).
- **The action colour rule (2026-09-26, owner decision, not a roadmap task, applied before `/review-phase 2`):** red = the one commit per layer, destructive = red outline until confirmed by a named red button, secondary = neutral outline (≥ 3:1), triggers that open a form or a confirmation = `strong` (neutral solid: the FABs, Approvals row Approve and Approve all N), red never decorative (neutral focus ring, nav and selection; exceptions: error text with an icon, the count badges). PRODUCT §2 principle 12, ARCHITECTURE §14.1 spec, CLAUDE.md Definition of Done. Enforced by a lint rule (`BUTTON_COLOUR_SELECTORS`) and an e2e auto-fixture (`e2e/fixtures.ts`, every spec) that fails any test where a layer shows two visible `primary` buttons; its first run found /settings/days-off (Save days off + Add holiday), fixed by the owner's choice: "Add holiday" is a `strong` trigger opening a dialog whose "Add holiday" is the red commit. Also: `ConfirmDialog` / `ReasonDialog` require a label naming the action; `ErrorText` puts an icon on every error; the phone sticky bar is elevated (card surface, border, shadow). Visual sweep: 27 screens and dialogs at 1280, 430, 375, light and dark. **Android status bar fixed** (a black bar with dark icons in light mode): the manifest has no `theme_color`, trade-off accepted (ARCHITECTURE §14, "The Android status bar"); the phase-1 note that the glyphs follow the manifest colour's luminance was wrong. **Reinstall the app on the S23 to see it** (an installed WebAPK keeps its manifest until Chrome refreshes it).
- **Local Playwright workers: 3** (owner decision 2026-09-26). The 4-worker failures (`team.spec` :82, :127) are evidenced as host resource exhaustion (below), not app causes; CI's clean runner stays the authoritative full-suite proof.
- **Local full-run stalls (2026-09-26, root-caused to the machine, not the app):** `team.spec.ts:127` (the Owner's edit saved, the People table refreshed after the 5 s expect) and `:82` (the invitee's set-password action still "Saving…" at 5 s) failed once each in local full runs, never in CI and never in isolation (4 × 14 passed, 2 × 93 desktop passed). The trace shows the set-password POST waiting **6.2 s** on the server while GoTrue logged `PUT /user` at **2.03 s** (a bcrypt hash, normally ~0.1 s): the process was starved, not blocked. A sampled full run showed host available memory down to **965 MB** (avg 1.8 GB) with CPU averaging 71%, and twice in a row Windows refused to start Turbopack's PostCSS worker (`0xc0000142`, DLL init failed: resource exhaustion), which then cleared by itself. So: transient host resource exhaustion under 4 local workers (owner decision 2026-09-25). No timeout raised, no retry. Next measure if it recurs: a full run at 3 workers, memory floor and stall count compared (owner's call). Also found in that run and fixed: `EditableRecord`'s heading row did not wrap, so at 200% text on 375 px "Edit profile" reached past the card (a ~5 px margin, so it depended on font timing).
- **2.9 review fix (owner's check, 2026-09-26): action rows had a band on desktop.** `StickyActions` kept `md:bg-background`, a page-coloured band across the /me card behind Cancel and Save (dark mode showed it most); dialog footers had a `bg-muted/50` band with a border on desktop. Fixed in the shared pieces: phone = card / sheet surface + top border + safe areas; `md`+ = plain right-aligned row, Cancel then the action. Checked every use (/me edit, the Save confirmation, Request leave, Settings → Company and Thresholds, the day gate) at 1280, 375 and 430 in light and dark (computed styles and screenshots); `edit-pattern.spec.ts` asserts no band at desktop (proved failing on the old classes: `rgb(250, 250, 249)`).
- **2.9 CI findings (2026-09-25, CI #76 on `bb8eb5e`):** (1) **`/people/loading.tsx` wrapped `/people/[id]`** (a `loading.tsx` covers its page and every child segment), so a tap that beat the prefetch (a board row on a freshly loaded Today) flashed the People list's skeleton before the person; `motion.spec` "never shows the People list's skeleton" caught it once hydration got faster. Fixed at the root: the list page and its skeleton live in the `people/(list)/` route group (URLs unchanged). **Rule: a list's `loading.tsx` goes in a route group when the list has child routes.** (2) `owner-review.spec.ts:153` failed because the run crossed **midnight IST** (18:22–18:30 UTC): the spec's days belong to the start date and `todayIST()` had moved on. Not a code defect; the global teardown flags such runs. If it recurs outside midnight, root-cause it then.
- **2.9 leftovers (2026-09-25):** (a) refresh on return during edit mode is covered by a unit test (`shouldRefresh` with `editing`), not an e2e (the 30 s throttle); (b) the People sheet still carries "Attendance & leave" although the card itself now opens that page: drop it if the owner finds it redundant; (c) `useLeaveGuard` holds only link clicks: a `router.push` from code while an edit is dirty is not guarded (none exists today); (d) only one editor can be in edit mode at a time on a page by design: two `EditableRecord`s would each guard, which is fine, but each has its own Save.
- **2.8 review leftovers (2026-09-25, architecture review, not blocking):** (e) `BackLink` / `ViewLink` put `ref={markLive}` after `{...props}`, so a caller's ref would be replaced (none passes one today; merge refs if a Radix `asChild` trigger ever wraps them); (f) after a reload `tab-history` resets `pushedFromHome`, while the pre-hydration script reads the entry beneath from the Navigation API: seeding `pushedFromHome` the same way would remove the documented spare `[home, home]` entry; (g) no test proves `instrumentation-client` and the app chunks share one `core/observability/client` module instance (they do today): an e2e check that `client-sdk` is requested once would pin it.
- **2.8 leftovers (2026-09-25):** (a) the Worker upload carries `resvg.wasm` and `yoga.wasm` (next/og, ~1.4 MB) although nothing renders images: find what pulls them and drop them if unused; (b) `shadcn` (a CLI) and `cn` are in `dependencies`: check neither reaches a bundle and move them to `devDependencies` or remove; (c) sonner (37 KB) and the More sheet's Radix dialog are in every signed-in first load: lazy-mount them if the budget gets tight; (d) hydration is framework-bound (see the 2.8 handoff): the next real gain is less client work in the shell (a server-rendered bottom bar with a small client island) or React's selective hydration of the shell's parts, worth measuring only if a phone shows the gap.
- **First-glance audit (PRODUCT §2 principle 11, 2026-09-24; nothing fixed, each gap has its task):** (1) **Owner Today** shows the whole people board on the first screen: the first application (PRODUCT §4.7, **6.2**). (2) **A person's attendance and leave is three taps from its tab for the Owner** (More → People → card → "Attendance & leave" in the detail sheet): a People card opens `/people/[id]` directly for `attendance.view_all`, actions behind a secondary control (**2.9**). (3) **`/people/[id]` opens on the request list, not the person's today**; a one-line today status above the tabs (**6.2**). (4) **`/me` edits the profile inline** on the first screen; view first, Edit one tap deeper (**2.9**, the edit pattern's first screen anyway). (5) **6.1 (Staff My Day) is unbuilt**: its sections (pending acknowledgement, today, overdue, changes requested) are the exceptions themselves and must stay rows, never collapse to counts; upcoming and events may collapse. Conforming already: Approvals (exceptions as rows, two actions, Approve all on the group header, count on the tab), Settings (a list of sections, each one tap deeper), `/leave` (requests first, the month one tap deeper, Request leave on the first screen), the gate, the attendance strip and the Log out row.
- **2.4 follow-ups:** (a) **data for 2.6:** `back-gesture.spec.ts:259` ("logout: back from the sign-in page does not return to the app") is flaky on the **committed** code too: with `--repeat-each=4` on `mobile` + `mobile-lg` against the pre-2.4 tree it failed 8 of 11 (back landed on `/my-day` or `/login?next=%2Fmy-day`), and it failed in 2 of 5 full runs during 2.4. Repeats share one seeded person, which inflates the rate, but single full runs fail too. Not a 2.4 regression; the logout replace racing the overlay back-out is the first suspect. **Same family, once:** `auth.spec.ts:75` (the Admin logging out from the account menu) stayed on `/today` in one full run on 2026-09-24 and passed 5 of 5 repeated alone right after. (b) Approvals has no Realtime yet (5.1): a second device sees decisions on its next navigation. (c) Filter chips above ~20 waiting items (PRODUCT "Approvals") are not built; nothing reaches 20 in the pilot yet. (d) The Owner pays two head counts per page load for the badge: measure in 2.8 with the bundle work. (e) A `CONFLICT` on approving leave names the clash and the review sheet links to the person's leave; a one-tap "cancel that one" from the error was not built (2.2 follow-up c, partly closed). (f) From the 2.4 architecture review, kept for later: `BackLink`'s label says "Back to People" even when it goes back to Today (the board); a generic "Back" label when going back is the likely answer, decide with 2.7's motion work. The 2.3 diff does not touch `public/`, the worker or its registration. 2.2's last full run (194/194) was green, so something time- or load-dependent changed; **it is now the first thing 2.6 should look at, and until then the local full e2e run is red on this one test.** Suspect: under a loaded run `navigator.serviceWorker.ready` resolves before the worker **controls** the reloaded page. Check `navigator.serviceWorker.controller` after the reload before going offline. (d) Showing the Owner's reason on a **replaced** (superseded) row needs `leave_owner_edit` to write `decision_reason` on the original (it keeps it only in the audit meta today); until then a replaced row shows no reason (architecture review, 2026-09-24). - **2.2 follow-ups:** (a) ~~`leave_owner_edit` does not return the kept dates~~ **closed in 2.4** (`(new_id, kept_dates)`, named in the edit toast); (b) a change request approved over a day that a *system* correction linked to its superseded original (not a gate leave) keeps the old link: apply_leave only relinks days of a superseded **gate** request (scope of 2.2), so revisit if the pilot shows it; (d) **client bundle (2.8):** `modules/attendance/index.ts` exports the client `DayChoiceForm` beside `TodayAttendanceCard` (whose Overtime / Working-today buttons are client dialogs), so `/attendance` likely ships the card's dialogs and `/my-day` / `/today` the gate form; ARCHITECTURE §3.1 says no client components from a barrel while lint allows modules only through `index.ts`: measure in 2.8 and decide on per-screen entry points; (e) `today/loading.tsx` awaits `getCurrentMember()` to decide whether to draw the attendance card skeleton, which may cost a query before the fallback paints on a client-side navigation (review NIT, 2026-09-24): measure in 2.8 with the rest of the bundle work; (c) an approved `source = owner` request (from a correction to half day / leave) still makes `leave_decide` CONFLICT, by owner decision, with the clash named; the 2.4 dialog should offer "cancel that one" from the error.
- **iOS system text size (tied to 6.6, owner decision 2026-09-25):** an installed iPhone app does not follow the phone's text size, because iOS passes it to a web app only on opt-in. Opt in (`font: -apple-system-body` on `html`, then set the scale from it), check every screen at iOS larger text, then extend the zoom lock (`STANDALONE_SCRIPT`) to iOS. Until then an installed iPhone app keeps pinch-zoom as the accessibility safety valve.
- **Large text, what is left (2.7b sweep):** at 200% long names still end in an ellipsis (at least 74 px shows on every checked screen, which the spec allows); the loading skeletons do not wrap like the rows they trace, so under very large text a row can shift when data arrives. Revisit after the S23 check if either reads badly.
- **Server log noise in e2e:** `next start` logs "Error: The destination stream closed early" when a test ends while a page's RSC stream is still open. Already on the committed code (11 lines in `mobile.spec.ts` before 2.7b); harmless, but it buries real server errors in a failing run. Could be filtered in the Playwright web-server output.
- **Most bottom sheets have no swipe-to-dismiss.** The select sheet has one since the 3B review fixes (`core/ui/overlay/swipe-dismiss.ts`, no dependency); the other sheets and the phone dialogs still close by the ✕, the overlay, Escape or back. Adopt the hook in `SheetContent` / `MODAL_SURFACE` (a form sheet needs care: its content scrolls and holds inputs) after the phase-3 phone walk.
- ~~**The client close reason is hidden on screen, not in the database**~~ **Done in `/review-phase 3`** (owner must-fix, 2026-09-27): migration `20260927143324_client_close_reason_private.sql` moves the reason to the Owner-only `client_close_reasons` (keyed by the `closed` activity entry; existing reasons moved, `meta.reason` stripped); pgTAP `20` proves Owner allowed, Admin/other Admin/Staff denied, and that the deactivation reason still stays the Owner's.
- **3B review fixes, "later" items (2026-09-27, architecture-reviewer on `f8248ac..bcee20f`; the must-fix and should-fixes were fixed in the review-fix commit):** (a) the select trigger's `aria-controls` names the Radix list, which never mounts on a phone; point it at the sheet's list while the sheet is open. (b) The sheet's rows have no arrow-key handling (Tab only), for a keyboard on a narrow window. (c) Items render twice (the off-document Radix list and the sheet): children with ids or effects are duplicated; harmless today. (d) React events from the portaled sheet bubble through the React tree: a Select inside a clickable card (`DataTable` `onRowClick`) would get the row's click; none of the 11 callers does; stop click propagation at the sheet root if one ever does. (e) pgTAP 19's `pgtap_later_consumer` has no RLS (rolled back; would trip a catalog-wide "every table has RLS" sweep in the same transaction). (f) Every later FK to `files` needs an index (written in DATA-MODEL §9) for phase 5's `submission_items`.
- **`DataTable`'s mobile cards carry no selection checkbox**, so `BulkBar` has no phone story yet. Nothing selects rows before **4.5** (the Approvals inbox, bulk approve or reject); that task has to add it — a checkbox on the card and a bulk bar that clears the bottom nav and the safe area.
- **`StatusDot` is only used by People.** Every later list that shows state on a phone should use it rather than a full-width badge column (ARCHITECTURE §14.1).
- **Settings is not finished, by design.** The Thresholds screen leaves out `workload_warning_threshold` (4.3) and `default_task_reminders` (5.3), and `ListManager` only edits an entry's **name**, although `list_items` also carries `description`, `color`, `icon` and `meta`. 4.1 (task types: kind, calendar flag, location flag, reminders) is the task that has to extend the manager — either with a per-list field set or by giving the list its own form — rather than bolting fields onto the job-title screen.
- ~~**The company logo and the avatar are still missing from Settings** (`organizations.logo_file_id`, 3.3).~~ **Closed in 3.3** (`CompanyLogoCard` on Settings → Company, `AvatarEditor` on `/me`).
- **Sentry stack traces are not symbolicated on the Worker.** Frames read `worker.js:<line>`: `withSentryConfig` uploads (and deletes) source maps for Next's chunks during `next build`, but OpenNext then re-bundles them into `.open-next/worker.js` without a map, and that file's debug id matches nothing uploaded. Fix in a deploy-workflow task: keep Next's maps (`deleteSourcemapsAfterUpload: false`), build the Worker with a source map (check `opennextjs-cloudflare build` / esbuild options), then `sentry-cli sourcemaps inject` + `upload` on `.open-next/` with the release set to the commit SHA, and delete the maps before `wrangler deploy`.
- **Sentry cosmetic:** `server_name: localhost` on Worker events. (`user.geo` is stripped since 1.2.)
- **`Sidebar` keeps an optional `footer` prop** that nothing passes since the preview switcher went (1.2). Drop it or use it for the first real footer. (`MobileNavSheet` had the same prop and was deleted in 1.5.)
- **Security headers on the proxy's redirect responses on the Worker** are a ROADMAP 6.6 item (production hardening), not tech debt: staging `/today` 307 carries none of them, pages do, and only a check against the deployed Worker can prove the fix.
- **CI runs twice per PR push** (`push: phase-*` plus `pull_request`, different concurrency keys). Drop the `phase-*` push trigger now that branch protection requires the PR checks, or skip the push run when a PR exists.
- **`package.json` `engines.node` is `>=20.9.0`** while CI, deploy, `@types/node` and Vitest 5 assume Node 24. Raise to `>=24` so a wrong Node fails at install, not at `pnpm test`.
- **Component tests (jsdom + Testing Library) aren't set up.** 0.3's unit tests cover pure logic (nav, tones, contrast, reason validation, toast copy); the composites are verified through the `/dev/ui` gallery, screenshots and the 0.4 Playwright specs (404 page, theme toggle, sidebar nav, Staff bottom nav). Add jsdom only if a composite grows logic worth testing in isolation.
- **`ErrorState` renders its title as a `<p>`**, so 404/error screens have no heading for assistive tech or tests to target (`e2e/shell.spec.ts` asserts on the `alert` role instead). Consider an `<h2>` in the 10.2 accessibility pass.
- **The `check` and `e2e` CI jobs both install and build**; if CI minutes ever matter, share a build artifact between them. The `e2e` job also starts a Supabase stack (~1 min).
- **`billed` / `not_billed` sit in core's `STATUS_TONES`** on purpose: they're DATA-MODEL §0 enum values with no amounts or queries, and the badge is rendered only inside `modules/revenue` (ADR-0007). If that ever feels wrong, `StatusBadge` takes a `tone` override and revenue can own the mapping.
- **`themeColor` in `app/layout.tsx`** and `theme_color` in the manifest follow the light background / `prefers-color-scheme`, not the in-app theme choice. Drive the meta tag from next-themes in the 10.2 polish pass if the installed app's title bar looks wrong in dark mode.
- **Sentry release names:** `withSentryConfig` derives the release from git when uploading source maps; nothing sets `release` in `sentryOptions`, so events from a build without an upload carry no release. Add `NEXT_PUBLIC_RELEASE` (the commit SHA from the workflow) if release tracking becomes useful.
- **`wrangler.jsonc` has no Images binding**: `next/image` is unused. When logos arrive (3.4), either add the `images` binding (Cloudflare Images, free allowance 5k/month) or set `images.unoptimized`.
- **Unread dot on the bell** (5.1) uses the `brand` token per ARCHITECTURE §3.3.
- `src/modules/**` (and the unfilled `src/core/*` areas) are empty `.gitkeep` folders. Each task deletes the `.gitkeep` of the folder it fills; the skeleton mirrors what `eslint-plugin-boundaries` enforces.
- No `LICENSE` file (the repo is private and unlicensed). Add one only if that ever changes.
- ~~**`member_directory` must be recreated** in 3.3 for `avatar_file_id`~~ **done in 3.3** (`avatar_file_id` appended after `job_title_id`); the 4.1 task-visibility rule ("names of people on my tasks") extends its WHERE (columns only ever appended).
- **Phase 1 review, "later" items (2026-09-23):** `members` still has a direct INSERT path for `team.manage` (`members_insert_manage`; `member_invite()` is the sanctioned door) — revoke INSERT from `authenticated` in the next `members` migration, as `session_events` does; `member_accept_invite()` flips `invited → active` for any invited session before a password exists (own account only; add `if encrypted_password is empty then INVALID_STATE`); `session_login()` / `session_logout()` take `user_agent` and `ip_hash` from the caller, so `ip_hash` is not evidence — **2.1 attendance derives from `at` (server clock) only** (say so in DATA-MODEL §1); `/auth/confirm?token_hash=…` puts a one-time token in the query string that Workers Logs record (inherent to email links; the token dies on first use; record the exception in ARCHITECTURE §18.2); `inviteMember`'s rollback calls `deleteAuthUser` even when `issueInviteToken` reused an existing sign-in through the `recovery` fallback (the FK protects members' sign-ins; return a `createdHere` flag as `scripts/bootstrap-owner.mjs` does); `updateListItem` / `archiveListItem` update by id alone and ignore the validated `listKey` (add `.eq("list_key", listKey)` before a per-list permission arrives in 4.1/7.4); `logout` rethrows a `session_logout` error before `signOut`, so a database outage leaves people unable to sign out (sign out regardless, report the failure); `createListItem` reads the last position then inserts, so two Admins adding at once get equal positions and `list_item_move()` then swaps equal values (same fix as the swap tie: a per-`(org_id, list_key)` unique index on `position` + retry, or an insert function); no activity-log policy yet covers `list_items` / `holidays` entities (Admins edit job titles but cannot read those audit rows; add when `core/activity` ships); `listMembers()` / `listDirectory()` are unpaginated (fine for a team this size; revisit past a few hundred rows).
- **Team follow-ups (1.3; 1.4 closed the archived-job-title and unused-`core/lists`-caller items):** `issueInviteLink` writes the `invite_link_issued` audit row before GoTrue answers (the state check must come first; a GoTrue failure leaves an audit row for a link that was never issued); `list_items_select` lets every active member read every `list_key` (revisit when an Owner-only list arrives); a "Resend invite email" button once Resend has a domain (today the copied link is the path); the People table scrolls horizontally at 375px (Owner/Admin screens are desktop-first; Staff never see it); a tester who never accepts leaves an `auth.users` row behind after "Revoke invite" (harmless: reactivate returns them to invited, and `email_exists` stops a re-invite).
- **`activity_log.actor_id` and `session_events.member_id` reference `members(id)` with no `on delete` rule**, so `records.hard_delete` of a member (or of the organization) fails on the FK. Decide in the hard-delete task whether member rows are ever deletable; if not, document it.
- **Members pgTAP fixtures insert `auth.users` rows directly** (id + email only). If a Supabase upgrade adds a not-null column there, `01_team_identity.test.sql` is the first thing to break.
- `core/errors`: add SQLSTATEs `23P01`, `22023`, `P0002` when tables that raise them exist. (The supabase `AuthError` branch arrived in 1.2: `core/errors/auth.ts`.)

## Open questions for Pixora
- **Late End day (3bA review, 2026-09-28):** **Settled at the phase 3b review (owner, 2026-09-28):** yes, a cutoff, `org_settings.end_day_cutoff_time` (default 05:00 IST, Settings → Thresholds); see "Phase 3b review decisions" (1). The question as asked: an End day after midnight lands on yesterday's open day (decision 7, the 2.x late-logout rule) with no cutoff, so a member who forgot and taps End day at 9:30 the next morning records yesterday ending at 9:30 and clears "End of day not recorded". The confirmation says so plainly ("End yesterday's day? … The end time recorded is now"), and once today is started yesterday can no longer be ended. Should there be a cutoff (e.g. until the 00:00 job flags it, or until a set hour)? Not blocking.
- **Gate route removed from the 3b app (2026-09-28):** **Confirmed at the phase 3b review, and the database side was removed by 3c's contract migration.** The question as asked: decision 28 lists "the day-gate route, its cookie" among what goes after the merge; 3bA removed the route and cookie handling from the 3b **app** only (the database side is untouched, `main` keeps its own copy), as the run-phase brief reads it. Confirm at `/review-phase 3b`.
- **Working hours, late starts and leaving early (Anna's request; decided by the owner and Anna 2026-10-07; not built, not on the roadmap yet: a small feature through `/add-feature` right after phase 6's release, before phase 8).** (1) **Working hours 10:00–18:00 IST on every working day, Saturday included** (weekly offs and holidays unchanged; both times are Owner-editable settings, never code). (2) **A late start is never blocked:** Start day always works; after a grace of 15 minutes (a setting) the day is marked "Late" with the minutes and a one-line reason, with no approval; the Owner sees a "Late today" count on Today, the names in the end-of-day report and a late-days count in the month summary; a "Start your day" reminder at the start time goes to anyone not started and not on leave. **The end time stays 18:00 for a late starter** (no make-up time; the Owner or an Admin uses "Stay until" when needed). (3) **A person's end time for a day** is 18:00, or the end time of a shoot (an event task with `event_end_at`) they are on that day, or a "Stay until" time set for them that day. (4) **"Stay until" can be set by the Owner and by Admins**; the person is notified at once and the strip shows it. (5) **Before their end time, End day becomes "Ask to leave early"** with a short reason; they stay checked in while waiting. **Only the Owner decides** (invariant 7), an Admin's own request included: Approve ends the day at the time they asked; "Stay" tells them to continue until their end time, they are reminded then, and End day is one tap again. **Unanswered after 30 minutes, they may end the day, marked "Left early, not answered"** for the Owner's review. (6) **The recorded end time is always the real tap**; nobody types a time; when the Owner decides doesn't change it. Leave planned ahead stays half-day leave; leaving without ending the day stays "End of day not recorded". The app records an early exit; it can't stop one. **Also decided (owner, 2026-10-07):** (a) a shoot only moves the end time **later**, never earlier: a shoot ending at 15:00 leaves 18:00 as the end time, and leaving earlier is an early-leave request; (b) an Admin's "Stay until" is for **Crew only**; the Owner's is for anyone; (c) **half-day leave records which half**: Morning (10:00–14:00) or Afternoon (14:00–18:00), so a morning half day is never Late and an afternoon half day is never an early leave (the hours follow the working-hours settings); (d) **a weekly off or a holiday has no Late or early-leave rules**: work on those days stays a day-off work note.
- **Tasks for the Owner (owner request and decision 2026-10-07; not built, not on the roadmap yet).** Today the Owner is never an assignee (WORKFLOWS §3, `task_create` refuses it). **Decided:** the Owner and Admins can put the Owner on a task, alone or with others; a task whose primary owner is the Owner completes on Done (nobody above the Owner); as one assignee of several, the normal route; the Owner gets "Task Noted" and the before-due, due and overdue reminders but no escalations; a "My tasks" section on the Owner's Today; Crew see it only when on the same task; Admins see the Owner's shoots as "Busy" on the calendar. The Crew can't assign the Owner (they only suggest tasks). Built with the item above through `/add-feature`, right after phase 6's release; it changes WORKFLOWS §3's "the Owner is never an assignee" rule and `task_create` / `task_update_assignment`, so it needs WORKFLOWS, PERMISSIONS and invariant-5/6 wording reviewed first.
- See PRODUCT.md **§8** (Admin metrics scope before phase 9; data to import at launch). §7 holds the settings already decided.

| 2026-10-01 | `sticky-actions.spec:87` red once on main's CI after PR #34 (run 36879970598, `[mobile]`, 200%: "Job title" 20 px behind the bar; owner: reproduce, establish what a person sees, fix it where it belongs; Opus 5.5, cloud session, on the **v1.3.1 fix branch `fix/no-surprise-reloads`** (from `main` after v1.3.0; not `phase-5`)) | one `fix(ui)` commit: `changeBottomReserve` (`core/ui/viewport/bottom-reserve.ts`, unit-tested) used by `StickyActions`, `PushBanner` and `OfflineBanner`; two regression tests in `sticky-actions.spec`, which now runs at 430 px too (`mobile-lg`); the spec's own measurement is main's, unchanged (an earlier draft moved its scroll inside the retry, which would have hidden the bug) | **Cause (app):** the bar's and the two bands' heights are published when measured; when one grows while the page is at its end, `main`'s padding grows with it but the page keeps its scroll position, so the bar moves up over the last field and stays there until the person scrolls again. CI hit it through the spec's 130% → 200% step, whose `scrollTo` landed before that frame's `ResizeObserver`: **reproduced deterministically** with the 200% change and the scroll in one task, covered **20 px on `[mobile]` (CI's exact number)** and 32 px on `[mobile-lg]`. **What a person sees** (measured every frame, real wheel scrolling): at 200% from the start, never covered at the end; changing the text size at the end leaves the page mid-form, and scrolling on reaches a clear end; **going offline at the end of a form covered the last field: 20 px at 100% (375 px), 120 / 80 px at 200% (375 / 430 px)**, until the next scroll. A late push-band chunk cannot do it: the chunk gates all hydration (Ideas, v1.3.1 sweep findings (a)), so Edit cannot be tapped before the band has measured. **Fix:** a person at the page's end stays at the end, in the same task as the change (before paint); a page that fits stays at its top, a page scrolled elsewhere is left alone (ARCHITECTURE §14.1). **Proof:** the two new tests fail without the fix (20, 20, 32, 80 px) and pass with it; the experiments after it: no covered frame, every end clear by 32–64 px; `sticky-actions.spec --repeat-each=20 --workers=1` on mobile + mobile-lg: 200 passed. **v1.3.0 verdict:** confirmed not a blocker (the field stays reachable by scrolling or by focusing it; CI saw it once), corrected in one respect: it was not test-only, a person who loses the connection at the end of a form saw the last field behind the bar until they scrolled. |

| 2026-09-29 | `/review-phase 3c` CI red: the Owner's /me at 200% on a 430px phone (Fable 5.1, cloud session) | **Root cause:** CI full run 36514578514 (`c4748c8`, `playwright (shard 2/2)`, `mobile-lg`) measured `me/loading.tsx` while it was still on screen: React 19.2 batches streamed Suspense reveals (`$RC` schedules the swap up to 300 ms after the previous reveal, and `load` does not wait for that timer), so on the starved runner the page's chunk landed a few ms after the loading screen's own and stayed hidden past `page.goto`; reproduced verbatim locally with a proxy that adds a 40–120 ms gap before the page's chunk: 130% green, 200% `["div","div","div[skeleton]","div","div[skeleton]"]`. The skeleton itself was the bug: its column wrappers had no `min-w-0`, so a `w-40` bar (320px at 200%) or a `w-52` (416px) set its column's minimum and the bar's `max-w-full` clamped to nothing; the avatar row (424px) and the Appearance and Sign out rows (416px) reached past a 430px phone, and past 375px. **Fix:** `min-w-0` on the columns (and `flex-wrap` + `shrink-0` where the page wraps) in `me/loading.tsx`, and the same pattern in `settings/custom-fields`, `leave/extra-work`, `people/[id]/leave`, the client overview and brand `loading.tsx` and `DetailSkeleton` (all measured overflowing at 200% before, none after; 25 loading screens swept at 375 and 430). `mobile.spec`: the 100% and large-text sweeps now wait for the settled page (`expectSettled`: header up, no `[data-slot="skeleton"]` left) and `LOADING_SCREENS` holds each loading screen on purpose (`heldShell` in `e2e/helpers.ts`: a pass-through proxy that delivers the route's skeleton and holds what streams after it) for /today (Owner, Admin) and /me (all three roles) at 100/130/200%, 375 and 430. Proof: `pnpm check` green (1013 unit in 82 files, pgTAP 1955 in 31 files, build, budget); full e2e after one `db:reset` at 3 workers **649 passed / 127 skipped / 0 failed, 11.9 min**; the CI full run dispatched on the push (id in the report) | Found in passing, not fixed (Ideas "3c review later items"): the settled `/settings/company` reaches past a 375px phone at 200%.
